<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的无声语音识别（SSR）方法受限于需要持续的面部附件、隐私问题和信号采集不稳定等问题，亟需一种更灵活、隐私友好且信号稳定的解决方案。

Method: 开发了一种佩戴在手上的柔软主动式EMG接口，集成液态金属（LM）互连、透明柔性印刷电路（FPC）电极和弹性体封装，确保手指运动时的机械稳定性；指尖电极可在需要时定位在嘴唇附近采集EMG信号，并使用深度神经网络进行信号分类。

Result: 深度神经网络在3名受试者对30词词汇的分类中实现了97.2 ± 1.3%的平均准确率，展示了强大的语言区分能力；实时无人机控制实验验证了该方法在嘈杂和隐私敏感环境中的实用性。

Conclusion: 柔软可穿戴EMG系统作为安全直观的人机交互界面具有巨大潜力，能够有效解决传统语音识别在嘈杂和隐私敏感环境中失效的问题。

Abstract: Silent speech recognition (SSR) provides an alternative communication pathway in the absence of audible speech. However, conventional approaches are limited by the need for constant facial attachment, privacy concerns, and unstable signal acquisition. Here, we propose a soft, active electromyography (EMG) interface that enables word-level SSR using machine learning. Worn on the hand, the device uses a fingertip electrode that can be positioned near the lips to acquire EMG signals only when needed. The interface integrates liquid metal (LM) interconnects, transparent flexible printed circuit (FPC) electrodes, and elastomer encapsulation to ensure high mechanical stability during finger motion. A deep neural network trained on these stable signals achieved a mean accuracy of 97.2 $\pm$ 1.3% across three subjects in classifying a 30-word vocabulary, demonstrating robust linguistic discrimination. Furthermore, real-time drone control validates the practicality of this approach in noisy and privacy-sensitive environments where conventional voice recognition fails. This study highlights the potential of soft, wearable EMG systems as secure and intuitive human-machine interfaces.

</details>


### [58] [Unifying Detection and Adaptation in Task-Free Continual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27070)
*Dezheng Han,Anbang Zhang,Zhihao Zhu,Shuaishuai Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FiUni框架，利用Fisher信息矩阵实现批量级任务检测和参数高效的持续学习，在无任务边界场景下达到与任务感知方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM持续学习方法依赖显式任务边界，限制了在真实无任务场景中的应用；而Fisher信息矩阵主子空间的正交性可以反映任务间的相似性。

Method: 基于K-FAC近似的FIM主子空间正交性构建冻结子空间指导LoRA适应；通过匹配新批次的Fisher主子空间与历史子空间，自适应决定复用、扩展或创建子空间。

Result: FiUni能有效推断批量级任务归属，在更少可训练参数下达到与先进任务感知CL方法相当的性能。

Conclusion: FiUni框架成功实现了无任务边界场景下的持续学习，通过Fisher信息引导的子空间管理动态平衡知识共享与任务隔离。

Abstract: To mitigate catastrophic forgetting in downstream continual learning (CL) for large language models (LLMs), existing methods typically constrain parameter updates or introduce task-specific adaptation modules. However, these methods often rely on explicit task boundaries during training, limiting their applicability to realistic task-free scenarios. In this paper, we propose a \textbf{Fi}sher-guided \textbf{uni}fied (\textbf{FiUni}) framework for batch-level task detection and parameter-efficient continual adaptation. FiUni is motivated by a key observation about the Fisher information matrix (FIM) of pre-trained models: the orthogonality among the principal subspaces of its Kronecker-Factored Approximate Curvature (K-FAC) approximation, estimated from a small number of downstream task samples, can reflect the similarity between different tasks. Based on this observation, FiUni constructs FIM-derived frozen subspaces to guide low-rank adaptation (LoRA), while matching the Fisher principal subspace of each incoming batch window with historical subspaces. This enables FiUni to adaptively determine whether to reuse existing knowledge, expand a related subspace, or create a new subspace, dynamically balancing knowledge sharing and task isolation. Experiments show that FiUni can effectively infer latent batch-level task affiliations and achieve competitive performance against advanced task-aware CL methods with fewer trainable parameters.

</details>


### [59] [Emotional Preferences as Goal-Priority Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27072)
*Shiqi Liu,Yihua Tan,Hu Fu,Guanyu Qi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个情感偏好涌现的计算框架，通过强化学习实现了高层次目标自主生成对竞争性低层次目标的偏好调节，证明了该方法能有效实现情境化的优先级切换、分级权衡和时间持续性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在变化的外部环境和演化的内部状态下，智能体的决策核心问题是：竞争性低层次目标的相对优先级是否可以由高层次目标自主生成的情感偏好来决定，而非外部预先指定。情感在调节竞争目标相对优先级方面发挥着重要的功能性作用。

Method: 基于目标导向的情感理论，提出了涌现性情感偏好的概念：高层次目标自主诱导对竞争性低层次目标的依赖状态的偏好。构建了一个包含多目标强化学习内部控制器和外部偏好生成器的框架：内部控制器提供偏好条件化的目标导向行为库，外部偏好生成器通过高层次目标的强化学习学习从当前状态到目标偏好的映射。将情感偏好操作化为通过优化涌现的相对目标优先级的依赖状态调节。

Result: 刻画了由偏好调节诱导的策略空间，推导了以内部行为库表示误差为指标的优化差距上界，证明当可用偏好条件化策略能表示最优策略时该差距消失。在自建的多目标探索环境实验中，学习到的偏好函数展现出情境优先级切换、分级权衡和时间持续性，优于评估的固定偏好和人工设计偏好策略。

Conclusion: 该研究成功地将目标导向的情感偏好理论转化为可计算的强化学习框架，证明了高层次目标能够自主生成对低层次目标的偏好调节，为智能体的情感化决策提供了理论基础和实证支持。

Abstract: A core question in decision-making for agents is whether the relative priorities of competing lower-level objectives can be determined by emotional preferences autonomously generated by higher-level goals, rather than being externally prespecified. Under changing external environments and evolving internal states, emotions play an important functional role in regulating the relative priorities of competing goals. Inspired by the goal-directed theory of emotion, this paper studies how such preference regulation can be computationally realized through reinforcement learning. We first propose a conception of emergent emotional preference: a high-level goal autonomously induces state-dependent preferences over competing lower-level objectives. This conception is built upon a framework consisting of a multi-objective reinforcement learning inner controller and an outer preference generator. The inner controller provides a repertoire of preference-conditioned goal-directed behaviors, while the outer preference generator learns a mapping from the current state to objective preferences through reinforcement learning on a high-level goal. We operationalize emotional preference as a state-dependent regulation of relative goal priorities that emerges through optimization. Furthermore, we characterize the policy space induced by preference regulation and derive an upper bound on the optimality gap in terms of the representation error of the inner behavioral repertoire. We show that the gap vanishes when the optimal policy can be represented by the available preference-conditioned policies. Experiments in self-constructed multi-objective exploration environments show that the learned preference function exhibits contextual priority switching, graded trade-offs, and temporal persistence, and outperforms the evaluated fixed-preference and handcrafted-preference strategies.

</details>


### [60] [Tabular Deep Learning for Algorithmic Trading: Cross-Regime Bayesian Optimisation for Equity Signal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27076)
*Joshua Le Grice*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种针对不同市场机制的超参数选择方法，通过贝叶斯优化训练五种模型类别，发现混合集成模型(XGBoost+TabNet)年化收益率达51.26%，夏普比率2.44，且信号精度在测试期间始终超越随机基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 算法交易市场规模超过200亿美元，信号稳健性的微小提升即可带来显著经济回报。然而现有股票预测模型评估在超参数选择时未明确针对市场机制稳健性，这正是本研究要解决的核心问题。

Method: 使用约300只美国大盘股11年的日频数据，训练五种模型类别，采用贝叶斯优化针对三个统计显著不同的市场机制进行超参数选择，通过排序聚合方法构建XGBoost和TabNet的混合集成模型。

Result: 机制稳健的超参数选择实现样本外泛化：信号精度在四个季度测试期均高于随机基准；混合集成模型年化收益51.26%，夏普比率2.44，CAPM alpha显著为0.423(p=0.011)，Beta接近零表明超额收益源于选股能力；另类数据作用次要用，且在空头端贡献更大。

Conclusion: 针对市场机制的稳健性优化能够提升模型泛化能力；无单一表格深度学习架构超越梯度提升树，但混合集成方法可产生更优表现；本研究开发了可实时探索结果的交互应用，仅差实时数据集成即可投入实际部署。

Abstract: Algorithmic trading now represents a market exceeding $20 billion, where even marginal gains in signal robustness can translate into economically significant returns. Existing evaluations of equity prediction models do not explicitly target regime robustness during hyperparameter selection. Five model classes are trained on daily observations from approximately 300 large-cap US equities over eleven years, with Bayesian optimisation configured to target trading performance across three statistically different market regimes. Regime-robust hyperparameter selection is associated with out-of-sample generalisation, as signal precision remains above the random baseline across all four quarters of the test period, and portfolio performance slowly degrades under simulated input noise before collapsing beyond a defined threshold. No individual tabular deep learning architecture outperforms gradient-boosted trees, but combining XGBoost and TabNet using rank aggregation produces a Hybrid ensemble with an annualised return of 51.26%, a Sharpe ratio of 2.44, and a statistically significant CAPM alpha of 0.423 (p = 0.011). A near-zero beta indicates this outperformance is driven by stock selection, not market exposure. Alternative data plays a secondary role once technical and fundamental features are accounted for, as well as contributing more strongly on the short side than the long, and varies by model class. An interactive application makes these results explorable in real time, with live data integration the remaining step toward practical deployment.

</details>


### [61] [Cone Extended Rayleigh Quotients for Directed Graph Learning: Minimax Spectral Certificates, Sensitivity, and Adaptive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27122)
*Yavdat Sh. Il'yasov,Nur F. Valeev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于双边锥Rayleigh框架的有向图学习谱分析与控制方法，在非对称传播算子上实现了谱认证、灵敏度分析和干预控制，并在Cora引用网络上实现了21.5%的谱水平降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有向图学习会产生可训练的非对称传播算子，具有不同的右谱和左谱结构。现有方法通常依赖对称性、非负性或锥保持性质，而这些约束在有向学习中并不满足。因此需要发展一种适用于非对称场景的谱分析、灵敏度计算和谱控制方法。

Method: 基于广义特征对[B_θ-λG]的两边锥Rayleigh框架，提出了面向学习的谱认证方法。使用可计算的上下锥界进行后验封闭，采用光滑软最小/最大代理保持严格单边界。推导了一阶灵敏度公式Dλ_C(B)[H]=v_C^T H u_C，实现了在给定扰动预算下的最优图支撑干预和自适应谱控制。

Result: 方法适用于非锥保持算子和有向学习场景。符号非对称扰动展示了从内部特征对到边界互补准对（包括非谱锥水平）的转换。在Cora有向引用网络上，自适应重新计算右-左灵敏度在0.5%的累积边权重削减预算下，将目标谱水平降低了约21.5%，且模型测试精度未观察到变化。

Conclusion: 提出的非对称谱分析框架克服了对对称性、非负性和锥保持的限制，为有向图学习提供了理论保障。实验证明了边方向携带的预测信息可被对称化移除，验证了方法的有效性和有向学习的必要性。该方法为图神经网络的谱控制和干预提供了新工具。

Abstract: Directed graph learning naturally leads to trainable nonsymmetric propagation operators with distinct right and left spectral structures. Building on the two-sided cone Rayleigh framework for generalized pencils \[ B_θ-λG, \] we develop a learning-oriented methodology for spectral certification, sensitivity analysis, and control without requiring symmetry, nonnegativity, or cone preservation. In the positive-orthant setting, computable lower and upper cone bounds provide an a posteriori enclosure of a distinguished cone level, while smooth soft-min/max surrogates preserve rigorous one-sided bounds with explicit approximation errors and remain differentiable with respect to the trainable parameters.
  For a simple interior level, the right and left modes satisfy \[ Dλ_C(B)[H]=v_C^T H u_C, \] yielding first-order optimal graph-supported interventions under prescribed perturbation budgets and motivating adaptive spectral control. Numerical experiments demonstrate the applicability of the approach beyond cone-preserving operators and in directed learning settings. Signed nonsymmetric perturbations reveal a transition from interior eigenpairs to boundary complementary quasi-pairs, including non-spectral cone levels, while controlled experiments show that symmetrization can remove predictive information carried solely by edge direction.
  On the directed Cora citation network, adaptive recomputation of the right--left sensitivity reduces the distinguished spectral level by approximately $21.5\%$ under a cumulative edge-weight reduction budget of $0.5\%$, with no observed change in test accuracy for the trained model and data split considered.

</details>


### [62] [TRACE-CRC: Trajectory-Adaptive Conformal Risk Control for Multi-Step Channel State Information Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27124)
*Kiarash Rezaei,Mehdi Sattari,Javad Aliakbari,Tommy Svensson,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TRACE-CRC方法，用于多步CSI预测中的轨迹感知不确定性量化，通过构建Frobenius范数不确定球并控制风险，实现了可靠的轨迹级覆盖率，同时避免了过度保守或覆盖不足的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度学习CSI预测器通常只提供点预测，缺乏校准的不确定性估计。在多步CSI预测中，预测目标是未来CSI矩阵序列，如果预测轨迹的任何部分不可靠，下游决策（如波束成形或调度）可能失败。这一局限性在多步预测场景中尤为严重。

Method: 提出轨迹自适应校准和基于保形风险控制的误差分析方法（TRACE-CRC）。该方法围绕预测的CSI矩阵构建Frobenius范数不确定球，并控制至少一个未来帧未被覆盖的风险。TRACE-CRC不是独立校准每个未来步骤，而是结合了依赖未来步骤的误差分析、轨迹难度分层和学习后测试（LTT）风险控制。

Result: TRACE-CRC实现了可靠的轨迹级覆盖率，其不确定球明显小于保守的多步校正方法，同时避免了紧凑分步和自适应保形基线方法的轨迹覆盖不足问题。

Conclusion: TRACE-CRC成功解决了多步CSI预测中的不确定性量化问题，在保证可靠覆盖的同时提高了预测效率，为下游无线通信决策提供了可靠的不确定性估计。

Abstract: Reliable prediction of time-varying channel state information (CSI) is essential for efficient wireless communication. Each CSI frame is a matrix-valued representation of the wireless channel response, and a sequence of CSI frames forms a temporal channel trajectory. Modern deep learning-based CSI predictors, however, often provide only point predictions and lack calibrated uncertainty estimates. This limitation is particularly problematic in multi-step CSI prediction, where the target is a sequence of future CSI matrices, and downstream decisions such as beamforming or scheduling may fail if any part of the predicted trajectory is unreliable. We propose trajectory-adaptive calibration and error profiling with conformal risk control (TRACE-CRC), a method for trajectory-aware uncertainty quantification in multi-step CSI prediction. TRACE-CRC constructs Frobenius-norm uncertainty balls around predicted CSI matrices and controls the risk that at least one future frame is uncovered. Instead of calibrating each future step independently, TRACE-CRC combines future-step-dependent error profiling, trajectory difficulty stratification, and learn-then-test (LTT) risk control. Empirically, TRACE-CRC achieves reliable trajectory-level coverage with substantially smaller uncertainty balls than conservative multi-step corrections, while avoiding the trajectory undercoverage of compact stepwise and adaptive conformal baselines.

</details>


### [63] [Ultra Low-Power, Lightweight, Probabilistic RSS-Based Path Reconstruction: A System for Landscape-Scale Bee Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27152)
*Christopher J. Noroozi,Joseph L. Woodgate,Michael Mangan,Michael T. Smith*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于RSS的新型低功耗定位方法，通过旋转高增益发射器和概率建模实现38mg超轻接收器的精准跟踪，在300m范围内达到10-15m精度，功耗低于600μW，成功应用于大黄蜂回巢飞行研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 运动生态学、物联网和机器人等领域需要对过小且功耗受限的设备进行定位，而传统GNSS无法应用于此类设备。现有的基于接收信号强度（RSS）的低功耗定位方法存在范围受限和需要大量RSS测量导致功耗高的问题，难以实现精准的角度估计（AoA）。

Method: 提出一种新颖的RSS定位方法：1）使用少量RSS测量，通过旋转高增益发射器实现300m覆盖范围；2）应用概率建模推断到达角度（AoA）；3）采用高斯过程对接收器运动路径建模；4）使用双重随机变分推断进行路径重建。

Result: 成功实现对38mg超轻接收器（含电源）的跟踪定位：在300m范围内达到约15m精度，功耗低于180μW；增加RSS测量量可提升至约10m精度，功耗低于600μW。系统成功应用于大黄蜂（Bombus terrestris）回巢飞行跟踪实验。

Conclusion: 该方法为飞行昆虫行为学研究等领域提供了有效解决方案，实现了超轻、低功耗设备的精准定位，在可扩展的景观范围内具有良好的应用前景。

Abstract: Applications in fields such as movement ecology, Internet of Things or robotics share the need for systems that localize devices that are too small and power constrained to implement GNSS (Global Navigation Satellite Systems). Alternative low-power localization methods often rely on only measurements of RSS (Received Signal Strength) to infer the AoA (Angle of Arrival) of a transmitted radio frequency signal, but are limited by range and the power demand of the large number of RSS measurements required to infer an accurate AoA. In this paper we address these issues with a novel RSS-based method for tracking ultra lightweight and low-power moving receivers across a complex landscape, achieved by using a minimal number of RSS measurements from simple rotating high-gain transmitters with a range of 300m, and applying probabilistic modelling to infer their AoA. The receiver's movement path is then modelled using a Gaussian process and reconstructed using doubly stochastic variational inference, resulting in approximately 15m accuracy tracking of receivers weighing 38mg (including power source) over a scalable landscape range while consuming less than 180uW, increased to approximately 10m accuracy at less than 600uW by taking more RSS measurements. We anticipate that this method will support fields such as the behavioural study of flying insect species, which we demonstrate by applying the system to track Bombus terrestris nest return flights.

</details>


### [64] [Inductive Correlation Clustering with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27153)
*Francesco Paolo Nerini,Francesco Bonchi,Arijit Khan,André Panisson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图神经网络(GNN)的归纳式相关性聚类(ICC)框架,解决了传统CC算法的可扩展性差和直推式局限,在推理速度提升5个数量级的同时保持竞争性聚类质量,并可作为图分类的可学习池化机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统相关性聚类(CC)算法存在两大关键局限:(1)可扩展性差,难以处理大规模问题;(2)本质上是直推式的,每次新问题实例都需从头执行算法。这限制了CC在实际应用中的灵活性和效率。

Method: 提出归纳式相关性聚类(ICC)问题设定,并利用图神经网络(GNN)构建学习框架。该框架在训练阶段学习利用图的结构模式和节点特征,从而能够泛化到训练时未见过的、来自相同分布的新图实例,实现高效的归纳式学习。

Result: 在归纳式设置下,推理时间降低高达5个数量级,近似比与最佳基线方法相差仅约10%;在标准直推式CC基准测试中也达到竞争性结果。此外,作为图分类的可学习池化层,有效增强了GNN捕获层次结构信息的能力。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将相关性聚类从直推式扩展到归纳式范式,通过GNN学习实现了效率与质量的有效平衡,为大规模、动态场景下的聚类应用提供了新思路,同时拓展了CC作为图神经网络池化机制的应用前景。

Abstract: Correlation Clustering (CC) is a natural formulation of clustering in combinatorial optimization, which uses a graph representation of the input and does not require a pre-specified number of clusters. Given $n$ objects and a pairwise similarity function, the goal is to cluster the objects so that similar objects are put in the same cluster and dissimilar objects are put in different clusters. Despite its versatility, existing CC algorithms suffer from significant scalability issues and are inherently transductive: i.e., the algorithm must be executed from scratch for any new problem instance.
  In this work, we bridge this gap by leveraging Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) to solve Inductive Correlation Clustering, a novel generalization of the CC problem designed to handle unseen graph instances. By learning to exploit common structural patterns and node features during training, our framework generalizes to new graphs drawn from the same distribution with minimal computational overhead with respect to standard algorithms. We demonstrate the effectiveness and scalability of our approach through extensive experiments. Our framework not only excels in the inductive setting, e.g., lowering the inference time up to $5$ order of magnitude, while maintaining an approximation ratio within $~10\%$ of the best baseline solution, but also achieves competitive results on standard (transductive) CC benchmarks. Finally, we showcase a practical application of our framework as a learnable pooling mechanism for graph classification. Our results indicate that our method serves as an efficient pooling layer, enhancing the ability of GNNs to capture hierarchical structural information in networks.

</details>


### [65] [Diffusion Policies for Short-Horizon Planning in Robot Crowd Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27158)
*Wendong Li,Jochen Garcke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了PDPO框架，利用扩散策略生成短时域动作块来提升机器人人群导航的安全性和效率，并修正了现有基准测试中的评估缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人群导航强化学习方法在每个时间步输出单一反应式动作，限制了其在密集、动态、多模态人机交互中表现多样化短期避障策略的能力。同时，现有基准测试存在评估缺陷：智能体可能通过离开有效区域来绕过密集人群。

Method: 提出规划扩散策略优化（PDPO）离线到在线强化学习框架：首先在避障演示数据上进行预训练，然后将去噪过程视为内部决策过程，使用PPO进行在线微调。执行时生成五步动作块并以滚动时域方式应用。同时引入新的评估设置，将边界违规视为碰撞。

Result: PDPO相比强基线方法获得了更高的成功率，消融实验表明动作块在修正后的有界基准测试中尤为重要。

Conclusion: PDPO框架通过扩散策略生成动作块有效提升了人群导航性能，修正后的基准测试设置更合理，能更好评估算法的真实能力。

Abstract: Robot crowd navigation requires safe and efficient decision-making under dense, dynamic, and multimodal human--robot interactions. Existing reinforcement-learning methods typically output a single reactive action at each timestep, which limits their ability to represent diverse short-term avoidance strategies. We propose Planning Diffusion Policy Optimization (PDPO), an offline-to-online reinforcement-learning framework that uses a diffusion policy to generate short-horizon action chunks for crowd navigation. PDPO is first pretrained on collision-avoidance demonstrations and then fine-tuned online with PPO by treating the denoising process as an internal decision process. During execution, the policy generates a five-step action chunk and applies it in a receding-horizon manner. Furthermore, we observe an evaluation artifact in common crowd-navigation benchmarks: without explicit boundary constraints, learned agents may leave the valid domain and bypass dense crowds. To address this, we introduce a setting in which boundary violations are treated as collisions. Experiments show that PDPO obtains an improved success rate over strong baselines, and ablations demonstrate that action chunks are especially important for the modified bounded benchmark.

</details>


### [66] [TraceBench: Controlled Evaluation of LLM Agents for Time-Series Root-Cause Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27182)
*Tommaso Bendinelli,Artur Dox,Christian Holz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TraceBench，一个基于模拟的框架，用于生成可控的根本原因归因任务，系统性地评估LLM代理在时间序列异常检测中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理越来越多地应用于真实世界系统的时间序列异常检测和根本原因分析，但其在受控条件下的性能尚未得到系统性评估。缺乏标准化的评估框架限制了对这些代理能力的深入理解。

Method: 提出了TraceBench框架，通过模拟物理动力学生成可控的根本原因归因任务。每个任务中，代理接收来自动力系统模拟的时间序列观测数据，需要判断系统参数是否在模拟过程中被改变以及哪个参数被改变。使用三个可解释的机械系统生成任务，并在受控实验条件下系统评估了四个LLM代理。

Result: 实验发现：(1)代理从领域上下文中获益显著；(2)代理主要通过数值控制台输出而非可视化来探索数据；(3)当要求代理生成将时间序列样本映射到预测根本原因标签的Python脚本时，其表现通常比直接提交预测更差。

Conclusion: TraceBench提供了系统评估LLM代理在动力系统时间序列分析能力的方法，揭示了这些代理在异常检测任务中的行为特点和局限性，为未来改进提供了重要见解。研究资源已在tracebench.github.io公开发布。

Abstract: LLM agents are increasingly applied to anomaly detection and root-cause analysis in time-series observations collected from real-world systems; however, their performance on these tasks has not been systematically evaluated under controlled conditions. We introduce TraceBench, a simulation-based framework for generating controlled root-cause attribution tasks. In each generated task, an agent receives time-series observations produced by simulating a physical dynamical system and must determine whether a system parameter was altered during the simulation and, if so, which one. Using TraceBench, we generate tasks from three interpretable mechanical systems and systematically evaluate four LLM agents across controlled experimental conditions, yielding new insights into how these agents analyze time-series observations from dynamical systems. Our results show that agents benefit substantially from domain context and explore data primarily through numerical console output rather than visualizations. We also find that agents generally perform worse when required to produce a Python script that maps each time-series sample to a predicted root-cause label than when they submit predictions directly. We release our datasets, agent trajectories, experimental results, and a leaderboard on our website, tracebench.github.io.

</details>


### [67] [When Interference Graphs Evolve: Doubly Robust Estimation of Dynamic Peer Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27187)
*Xiaojing Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个受控对比框架和DynaNet-DR估计器，用于在动态网络中估计同伴效应，解决了预分配网络历史、动态同伴暴露和后分配网络变化具有不同因果角色的难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同伴效应的估计在交互图谱随时间演化时面临困难，因为预分配网络历史、动态同伴暴露和后分配网络变化各自具有独特的因果作用，现有方法难以有效处理这种复杂的时序网络结构。

Method: 引入受控对比框架，将潜在结果索引于自身处理、时间聚合的同伴暴露和后分配演化摘要，定义自身处理、同伴暴露、受控网络演化和联合受控对比；开发动态网络双重稳健估计器DynaNet-DR，结合时间因子化倾向得分与标准化增强，实现当结果回归或倾向估计器任一一致时的双重稳健性。

Result: 在一致性、摘要充分性、序贯可交换性、正值性、 nuisance收敛和弱依赖假设下，规范估计器具有一致性；基于固定真实时序图序列的半合成基准测试显示，在针对完整轮廓的方法中具有更优的估计精度；MathOverflow研究展示了观测性应用的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 提出的受控对比框架和DynaNet-DR估计器为动态网络中的因果推断提供了理论基础和实用工具，能够有效区分并估计不同网络演化阶段的因果效应，在模拟实验和实际应用中均表现良好。

Abstract: Peer effects are difficult to estimate when interaction graphs evolve because pre-assignment network history, dynamic peer exposure, and post-assignment network change have distinct causal roles. We introduce a controlled contrast framework that indexes potential outcomes by own treatment, temporally aggregated peer exposure, and a post-assignment evolution summary. Differences between the resulting means define own-treatment, peer-exposure, controlled network-evolution, and joint controlled contrasts rather than a mediation decomposition. We develop the Dynamic Network Doubly Robust estimator, DynaNet-DR, which combines a temporally factorized propensity with normalized augmentation. Under consistency, summary sufficiency, sequential exchangeability, positivity, nuisance convergence, and weak dependence, its canonical estimator is consistent when either the outcome regression or the propensity estimator is consistent. The reported implementation adds representative-score prediction, fixed clipping, and finite-sample stabilization. Semi-synthetic benchmarks on fixed real temporal graph sequences show favorable estimation accuracy among methods targeting the full profile. These benchmarks assess summary-indexed contrasts rather than counterfactual edge generation, and the MathOverflow study is an observational illustration under the stated assumptions.

</details>


### [68] [Common Geodesics Do Not Guarantee Fisher Consistency of the Structured SVM: Minimal Counterexamples and a Tree-Metric Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27203)
*Jintao Fei,Jiangying Luo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现结构化支持向量机Fisher一致性的已知必要条件（任务损失为度量且每个输出三元组有公共测地线点）对于标准坐标级argmax解码器并不充分。给出了反例并完全分类了正权重树度量：当且仅当树为路径时保持argmax一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究表明Fisher一致性需要任务损失满足特定度量条件，但该条件是否充分尚不清楚。作者旨在验证此条件对标准argmax解码器是否充分，并探究在何种度量结构下能保证一致性。

Method: 构建四输出星形结构的反例证明条件不充分；完全分类正权重树度量，证明路径树保持一致性而分支树失效；在满足公共测地线条件的度量中寻找最小反例；对汉明立方体构造全支撑反例；所有最优性声明均有精确的原-对偶证书。

Result: 四输出是满足条件度量的最小反例规模；树度量的argmax一致性当且仅当树为路径；分支树的失效仅限于边界分布，全支撑分布下所有树都保持argmax性质；满足公共测地线条件的度量中，五输出是全支撑反例的最小规模，K_{2,3}是无限K_{m,n}家族的最小成员；给出三维汉明立方体的全支撑反例。

Conclusion: 揭示了具体的解码器差距：在多面体设定下，嵌入可以保证校准链接的存在，但无法验证每个代理风险最小化器上的指定argmax链接。已知必要条件对标准解码器不充分，一致性的充分条件更为严格。

Abstract: A known necessary condition for Fisher consistency of the structured support vector machine requires the task loss to be a metric for which every output triple has a common geodesic point. We show that this condition is not sufficient for the canonical coordinate-wise argmax decoder. A four-output unit star admits an exactly optimal score vector whose maximizers are all strictly non-Bayes, and four outputs are minimal among metrics satisfying the condition. We then completely classify positively weighted tree metrics whose vertex set is the output space: argmax consistency holds if and only if the tree is a path. The failure on branching trees is confined to boundary distributions; every tree retains the argmax property at every full-support distribution. Among metrics satisfying the common-geodesic condition, five outputs are necessary and sufficient for a full-support counterexample; $K_{2,3}$ is the smallest member of an infinite $K_{m,n}$ family. We additionally give a full-support counterexample for the three-dimensional Hamming cube. All optimality claims have exact primal-dual certificates. The counterexamples expose a concrete decoder gap: in this polyhedral setting, an embedding can guarantee the existence of a calibrated link without validating a prescribed argmax link on every surrogate-risk minimizer.

</details>


### [69] [Profit based evaluation of machine learning for nitrogen recommendations in winter wheat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27205)
*Xulong Wang,Po Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 机器学习无法替代标准氮肥施用建议，但作为标准建议的利润导向修正步骤，可将利润损失减少25-43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有氮肥施用建议在设定时未考虑价格波动，而近期价格剧烈波动使最优施氮量变化显著；机器学习方法虽常被提出作为解决方案，但其评价标准通常是预测准确度，而预测准确并不等同于施用建议能带来更高利润。

Method: 构建一个基于892条实测产量响应曲线的测试基准，通过改变氮肥与谷物价格比来模拟各种价格场景；直接以建议氮肥用量所导致的利润损失作为评价标准，而非传统的预测准确度。

Result: 机器学习模型无法在农场容差范围内恢复最优施氮量；在正常价格下，所有模型的利润均低于标准建议；简单的后处理修正步骤可使利润损失减少四分之一，且无需重新训练即可在第二个地点减少43%的损失；标准建议与阻尼修正的混合方法可消除偏差并减少罕见的大额损失。

Conclusion: 机器学习不应作为标准氮肥施用建议的替代品，而应作为其利润导向的修正步骤使用，这种应用方式才具有实际经济价值；同时，该方法也可用于评估减排成本，其成本与当前碳价格相当。

Abstract: Nitrogen rates for winter wheat are set before the season, under unknown prices and weather. The standard UK advice does not respond to prices, yet recent price swings moved the most profitable rate by tens of kilograms per hectare. Machine learning is often proposed as the fix. However, it is usually judged on prediction accuracy, and accurate prediction does not by itself make the recommended rate more profitable. Our insight is to score nitrogen advice directly by the profit it forgoes on measured yield response curves. We build a test bench on 892 such curves from two long running UK experiments, and sweep the nitrogen to grain price ratio to cover all price scenarios. On this bench, machine learning fails as a predictor. No model recovers the best rate within farm tolerance, and the benchmark noise shows none can. At normal prices, every model also loses to the standard advice on profit. The gain sits elsewhere. A simple correction step applied after the model cuts profit losses by a quarter, while better models and extra features give no gain. The same frozen correction cuts losses by 43% at the second site without any retraining. A hybrid of standard advice plus a damped correction removes bias and trims rare large losses. The same price sweep also prices emission cuts, at a cost comparable to current carbon prices. Machine learning therefore pays as a profit scored correction to standard advice, not as its replacement.

</details>


### [70] [HALO: A Heterogeneity-Aware Language-Aligned IMU Foundation Model for Open-Set Human Activity Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27233)
*Zihan Ding,Liyu Zhang,Xiaomin Ouyang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了HALO，一个面向IMU的领域基础模型，通过两阶段训练框架解决感知异质性和开放集识别问题，在零样本开放集识别任务上超越现有方法，且参数量仅为最新基础模型的十分之一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有人类活动识别研究缺乏能够跨不同受试者、设备和活动泛化的统一模型。主要挑战包括：(1)感知异质性——采样率、通道配置和传感器位置的差异；(2)对未见过的活动和标签词汇表的泛化能力差。

Method: HALO采用两阶段训练框架：第一阶段使用感知异质性感知的自监督学习预训练IMU编码器，包含自适应池化tokenization、通道无关特征提取和将自然语言传感器描述注入通道嵌入的情境化传感器条件化；第二阶段通过同义词感知软对比学习将IMU编码器与文本嵌入对齐，实现基于余弦相似度检索的开放集识别，无需针对每个数据集训练分类器。

Result: HALO在10个公共HAR数据集上训练，在7个留出数据集上评估，在所有8项聚合指标上均优于五个最先进基线。尽管仅有约35M可训练参数（比最新基础模型MOMENT的341.2M少10倍），HALO在所有87个训练标签上的零样本开放集准确率提高了13.7个百分点。在基线匹配输入设置下，HALO在4种设置中的3种仍保持领先。

Conclusion: HALO成功构建了一个高效的IMU基础模型，通过异质性感知和语言对齐解决了HAR领域的关键挑战，以更少的参数实现了更优的性能，证明了领域特定基础模型的优越性。但在存在严重分布偏移的两个数据集上，所有模型包括HALO在内的零样本性能均崩溃，表明极端场景下仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Human Activity Recognition (HAR) using inertial measurement units (IMUs) enables a wide range of applications, yet the field still lacks a unified model that can generalize across diverse subjects, devices, and activities. Training such a model is difficult due to two key challenges: sensing heterogeneity -- differences in sampling rates, channel configurations, and sensor placements -- and poor generalization to unseen activities and label vocabularies. We introduce HALO (Heterogeneity-Aware Language-aligned Open-set model), a domain-specific IMU foundation model that addresses both challenges through a two-stage training framework. Stage 1 pretrains the IMU encoder with heterogeneity-aware self-supervised learning, including adaptive-pooling tokenization, channel-independent feature extraction, and contextualized sensor conditioning that injects natural-language sensor descriptions into each channel embedding. Stage 2 aligns this IMU encoder with text embeddings via synonym-aware soft contrastive learning, enabling open-set recognition via cosine-similarity retrieval without per-dataset classifiers. Trained on 10 public HAR datasets and evaluated on 7 held-out datasets, HALO outperforms five state-of-the-art baselines on all 8 aggregate metrics, and still leads on 3 of 4 settings under baseline-matched inputs. Despite using only ~35M trainable parameters -- 10x fewer than the latest foundation model MOMENT (341.2M) -- HALO improves zero-shot open-set accuracy, measured over all 87 training labels, by 13.7 percentage points. On two further datasets with severe distribution shift, every model including HALO collapses zero-shot. A video demonstration of HALO's performance in real world is available at https://youtu.be/rooVKragtFU

</details>


### [71] [Importance Scoring of Transformer Attention Heads in Learning Tabular Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27241)
*Ahmad Jad Allah,Kazi F. Akhter,Md. Kamrozzaman Bhuiyan,Manar D. Samad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将重要性评分指标应用于表格数据的多头Transformer模型解释，发现移除低重要性注意力头时模型表现更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型具有计算量大和不可解释性的特点，而Transformer在表格数据上的应用研究较少，缺乏对其注意力头重要性的理解和优化方法。

Method: 提出一种注意力头重要性评分指标，在40个多样化的表格数据集上进行实验，通过逐步移除不同重要性的注意力头来评估模型性能变化。

Result: 在72.5%的实验中，逐步移除重要性最低的注意力头时模型性能下降最小；重要注意力头分散在不同层中，无特定层的集中趋势；表格数据领域注意力头的重要性在不同数据集间差异显著。

Conclusion: 所提出的重要性评分可有效识别Transformer架构中的冗余注意力头，有助于提高模型效率；注意力头重要性在表格数据领域具有独特性，与图像和语言领域不同。

Abstract: Computationally demanding and opaque deep learning models can be better understood and optimized by analyzing how they transform data. While deep transformers have been widely studied in computer vision and natural language processing, their application in tabular data remains relatively underexplored. This paper presents one of the first applications of an importance-scoring metric to interpret multi-head transformer models in learning from tabular data. Experiments conducted on 40 diverse tabular datasets demonstrate robustness to head drops based on the proposed head importance score. In 72.5\% of experimental examples, the model remains most resilient to performance drops when heads with the lowest importance scores are gradually removed. In contrast, removing the most important attention head first results in the greatest reduction in classification performance. A closer look at individual head importance scores across six attention layers reveals that important heads are scattered across layers, with no consistent layer-specific trends. In contrast to the image and language domains, the importance of individual attention heads varies considerably across tabular datasets with different schemas and feature spaces. The proposed importance score can improve efficiency and redundancy within transformer architectures. We make the source code for measuring the importance of individual attention heads publicly available.

</details>


### [72] [Circuit Condensation: Post-Training that Concentrates a Behavior's Causal Circuit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27254)
*Sai Adith Senthil Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出电路压缩方法，通过后训练将模型行为集中到更小的因果图中，平均缩小8.1倍，最大达316倍，并揭示了权重更新（而非仅搜索）是电路简化的关键驱动力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机制可解释性方法通过电路（组件和连接）解释模型行为，但冻结发现方法通常返回数百条边，难以全面检查、比较或验证。这促使研究者寻求一种能够将行为压缩到更小因果图的方法，以提高可解释性和可验证性。

Method: 提出电路压缩方法，迭代执行两个步骤：首先剪除低归因边，然后训练低秩适配器使剩余结构匹配原始模型表现。仅当任务性能和通用能力保持不变时才保留剪除操作。通过穷举测试所有电路子集来验证不可约简性，并使用配对消融揭示边之间的依赖关系。

Result: 在4种行为和8个模型的32个设置中，30个设置的压缩电路比最强冻结基线更小，平均缩小8.1倍，最大316倍。权重更新驱动电路缩小（29/32设置）。发现11个不可约简电路和可移除边。在间接宾语识别任务上，压缩电路仅含24个注意力头（17个有文档记录），而匹配的冻结电路有61个头（36个无文档），形成已发布机制的充分子电路而非重构。

Conclusion: 电路压缩成功地将模型行为集中到更小、更易解释的因果图中，证明了权重更新对电路简化的关键作用。压缩后的电路不仅追踪原始模型的下一词元分布并预测其错误，还提供了更清晰、更可验证的机制解释，为机制可解释性研究提供了新的有效工具。

Abstract: One approach to mechanistic interpretability explains behavior through circuits: the components and connections that carry it. Frozen discovery often returns hundreds of edges, making them hard to inspect, compare, or verify exhaustively. We introduce Circuit Condensation, which post-trains models to concentrate behaviors into smaller causal graphs. Each round prunes low-attribution edges and trains a low-rank adapter to match the original through what remains, retaining the cut only if task performance and general capability survive. Across four behaviors and eight models, condensed circuits are smaller than the strongest frozen baseline in 30 of 32 settings, by $8.1\times$ on average and up to $316\times$. Repeating the search without weight updates produces larger circuits in 29 of 32 settings, showing that weight updates, rather than search alone, drive the reduction. Testing every subset of 19 circuits finds 11 that cannot be reduced and reveals removable edges in the rest. Pair ablations expose dependencies between edges, showing that their effects cannot be understood independently. On indirect object identification, condensation isolates 24 heads, 17 of them with documented roles, against 61 heads and 36 undocumented ones for the matched frozen circuit: a sufficient sub-circuit of the published mechanism rather than a reconstruction of it. The resulting circuit tracks the original model's next-token distribution and predicts its errors.

</details>


### [73] [Making Latent Evolution Explicit: Operator-Structured Transitions for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27259)
*Xiaoxiao Lu,Yunlong Dong,Jiahao Shi,Ye Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了LEON网络，通过基于算子的传播和加性强迫在潜在空间建模演化，显著提升了世界行动模型在机器人策略中的闭环性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的潜在空间世界行动模型（WAMs）的归纳偏置集中于token交互而非时间演化，限制了其在动态系统建模中的有效性。研究者将转移实现作为一个独立于预测表示和预测-策略耦合的架构选择进行研究。

Method: 提出潜在演化算子网络（LEON），基于受控Koopman生成器视角，在学习的可观测空间中通过上下文调制的算子传播和加性强迫来建模潜在演化。该方法将上下文相关的转移变化组织在共享演化算子结构周围，同时保留加性变化的互补路径。

Result: 在受控动态系统上验证了演化特定的归纳偏置以及算子传播和强迫的互补作用。在两种不同集成潜在预测到策略的WAM框架中，LEON均提升了闭环性能和鲁棒性，且在完全转移替换下仍保持有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了转移实现在潜在世界行动模型中作为重要架构选择的地位，证明了专门针对时间演化设计的归纳偏置比通用Transformer架构更适合动态系统建模。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) augment robot policies by predicting how task-relevant scene states may evolve under interaction. Recent WAMs increasingly perform such prediction in latent representation spaces, avoiding full appearance-level generation while preserving control-relevant information. Yet latent transitions are commonly realized with Transformer-based predictors whose inductive structure is centered on token interaction rather than temporal evolution. We study transition realization as an architectural choice distinct from predictive representation and prediction-policy coupling. We introduce the Latent Evolution Operator Network (LEON), which models latent evolution in a learned observable space through context-modulated operator-based propagation and additive forcing. Grounded in the controlled Koopman generator view of evolution, LEON organizes context-dependent transition variation around a shared evolution-operator structure while retaining a complementary path for additive change. Controlled dynamical systems verify the resulting evolution-specific inductive bias and the complementary roles of operator propagation and forcing. Across two WAM formulations that integrate latent prediction into the policy differently, LEON improves closed-loop performance and robustness while remaining effective under full transition replacement. These results establish transition realization as a consequential architectural choice in latent WAMs.

</details>


### [74] [MM-Spectrum: Multimodal Multi-spectral Molecular Structural Elucidation with a Stable MoE Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27286)
*Hai-tao Yu,Nan Min,Zheng Fang,Hongyu Zhan,Yusen Tan,Yuhan Wang,Jun Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了MM-Spectrum框架，一种用于多模态多光谱分子结构推断的稀疏混合专家模型，通过模态感知路由机制解决多模态不平衡问题，在多种设置下显著提升了分子结构解析性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从多模态光谱测量推断分子结构需要整合互补但高度异构的信号。现有直接拼接多光谱序列的方法由于显著的异质性和由此产生的模态间不平衡，表现出异常的性能下降。

Method: 提出MM-Spectrum，一个稀疏混合专家框架：1）引入显式模态感知路由机制，将光谱身份暴露给路由器；2）设计共享专家和交互专家，配合异构专家容量，以提取模态独特信息和跨模态协同信息，同时抑制噪声干扰。

Result: 在全模态、双模态和缺失模态三种分子结构解析设置中，MM-Spectrum均取得了持续且显著的性能提升，并通过消融实验和可解释性分析提供了支持。

Conclusion: MM-Spectrum通过模态感知路由和异构专家设计有效解决了多模态光谱数据中的不平衡问题，为分子结构推断提供了一种有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Inferring molecular structures from multimodal spectroscopic measurements requires integrating complementary yet highly heterogeneous signals. However, the common paradigm of directly concatenating multispectral sequences can exhibit anomalous performance degradation, primarily due to pronounced heterogeneity and the resulting multimodal imbalance across modalities. As a remedy, we propose MM-Spectrum, a sparse Mixture-of-Experts framework tailored for multimodal multispectral spectra-to-structure elucidation. To better match the information characteristics under multispectral imbalance, MM-Spectrum introduces an explicit modality-aware routing mechanism that exposes spectral identity to the router in addition to token content representations. Moreover, it incorporates shared and interaction experts, together with heterogeneous expert capacities, to extract multispectral modality-unique and cross-modal synergistic information while suppressing noise-induced interference. Across full-modality, bimodal, and missing-modality settings on molecular structural elucidation, MM-Spectrum achieves consistent and substantial improvements, supported by ablation studies and interpretability analyses.

</details>


### [75] [QuantumBoostNet: A Hybrid Classical-Quantum Architecture for Enhanced Accuracy in Cardiac Ultrasound View Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27302)
*Mihai Udrescu-Milosav,Stefan-Alexandru Jura,Mihai Udrescu,Gerhard-Paul Diller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出了QuantumBoostNet，一种混合经典-量子架构模型，用于解决心脏超声视图识别任务，在噪声环境下表现优于现有最先进模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心脏超声视图中正确视角的准确识别对于精确解剖解读、可靠测量和减少临床错误至关重要。尽管计算机视觉技术显著进步，但现有最先进模型在专业医学影像任务中表现欠佳，主要原因是医学数据中存在的高水平噪声问题。

Method: 提出QuantumBoostNet混合经典-量子架构，包含经典骨干网络和两个输出头（一个经典头和一个量子头），量子头实现为参数化的10量子比特量子电路。采用两阶段训练策略，通过监控损失动态的混合参数控制头部之间的自适应转换。

Result: QuantumBoostNet在心脏超声视图识别任务中持续优于现有最先进的经典模型和混合经典-量子模型，实现了相对于最佳竞争者的相对改进。在标准图像分类基准测试中表现优越，并对噪声展现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持混合经典-量子模型在专业医学影像应用中的持续开发，证明了量子计算技术在医学影像分析领域的潜力和价值。

Abstract: Accurate identification of the correct view or angle in cardiac ultrasound (echocardiogram) is a critical component of cardiologic imaging. This step is essential for precise anatomical interpretation, reliable measurement, and the reduction of clinical errors. Although computer vision has advanced significantly, most state-of-the-art models perform well on standard benchmarks but often yield suboptimal results in specialized medical imaging tasks due to the high level of noise present in the data. QuantumBoostNet, a hybrid classical-quantum architecture, is introduced to address these challenges. This model integrates a classical backbone with two heads: one classical and one quantum, with the quantum head implemented as a parametrized 10-qubit quantum circuit. Training occurs in two stages, with an adaptive transition between heads governed by a mixing parameter that monitors loss dynamics. Extensive experiments indicate that, despite the limited number of qubits that can be simulated, QuantumBoostNet consistently outperforms state-of-the-art classical and hybrid classical-quantum models in cardiac ultrasound view identification, achieving a relative improvement over the best competitor. QuantumBoostNet also demonstrates superior performance on established image classification benchmarks and exhibits robustness to noise. These findings support the continued development of hybrid classical-quantum models for specialized medical imaging applications.

</details>


### [76] [Beyond Parallel Blindness: Information Floors and Model Gaps in Block Drafting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27339)
*Xinwei Qiang,Xiang Fang,Chang Chen,Yue Guan,Yufei Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种信息下界方法来分离块草稿生成中的两种拒绝损失，发现局部条件信息能消除86-100%的理论拒绝下界，但当前草稿器质量仍是主要瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块草稿生成中存在两种拒绝来源：块内路径信息缺失和可观测信息建模不完美，但传统的接受长度指标无法区分这两种来源，因此需要一种方法来分离理论下界和模型质量差距。

Method: 提出信息下界概念，即在特定条件阶数下的最小预期拒绝率；通过目标模型的前向展开来估计信息下界和模型差距；在四个领域、四个开源权重目标模型和一个前沿API目标模型上进行实验验证。

Result: 1) 全并行信息下界在Qwen3-4B最终位置达到0.286，限制最佳提案每位置接受率为71%；2) 一个已实现token即可消除86-100%的信息下界，证实了局部性特征；3) 当前草稿器远高于其理论下界：DFlash的最终位置模型差距占43-64%拒绝率，DSpark占85-92%。

Conclusion: 短距离条件信息的价值与提案质量可以被有效分离；信息下界为草稿器性能提供了理论上限参考；当前草稿器的主要瓶颈在于模型质量而非理论限制。

Abstract: Block drafters propose several tokens in one forward pass, before earlier target tokens are realised. Their rejection mixes two losses: missing within-block path information and imperfect modelling of observable information. Accepted length cannot distinguish them. We separate the two with an information floor, the minimum expected rejection at a specified conditioning order; rejection above this floor is the model gap. Estimating both from target rollouts across four domains, four open-weight targets, and a frontier API target yields three findings. First, the all-parallel floor reaches $0.286$ at the final slot on Qwen3-4B, limiting even the best proposal to $71\%$ per-slot acceptance. Second, one realised token removes $86$--$100\%$ of this floor, a locality also recovered by an independent mutual-information analysis. Third, current drafters remain far above their floors: the final-slot model gap accounts for $43$--$64\%$ of DFlash rejection and $85$--$92\%$ of DSpark's oracle-conditioned rejection. These findings separate the value of short-range conditioning from proposal quality.

</details>


### [77] [Understanding Evolution Strategies for LLM Reasoning: Broader Reasoning Coverage than GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27351)
*Yunpeng Ba,Zhi Zheng,Yue Xie,Jiaqing Li,Xialiang Tong,Tao Zhong,Mingxuan Yuan,Zhichao Lu,Xuyang Wu,Zhenkun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了进化策略(ES)在大语言模型推理后训练中的优化行为，发现ES相比主流的GRPO方法具有更广的推理覆盖率，能更好地利用预训练LLM的推理能力，并提出了一种结合两者的序列训练策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化策略(ES)作为一种内存高效的大语言模型推理后训练范式近年来受到关注，但其优化行为缺乏深入研究，难以明确其相比主流后训练范式(如GRPO)的优势范围，因此需要系统分析ES的动力学机制和性能特点。

Method: 1) 理论分析验证器投影的Jensen-Shannon多样性对Pass@K性能的影响；2) 实证对比ES与GRPO在Pass@1和Pass@K上的表现差异；3) 开发序列GRPO-ES训练策略结合两者优势；4) 分析ES中参数漂移与功能变化的关系；5) 研究超参数设计(如种群大小)对ES有效性的影响。

Result: 1) ES相比GRPO具有更广的推理覆盖率，在提高Pass@1的同时获得更高的Pass@K性能，而GRPO会出现熵坍塌；2) ES的性能增益仅来自于稀疏的大幅度参数更新子集，参数大幅移动不一定导致大范围功能变化或灾难性遗忘；3) 较大的LLM需要较小的种群规模；4) 序列GRPO-ES策略成功结合了GRPO在Pass@1上的优势和ES在Pass@K上的增益。

Conclusion: ES应被视为一种独特的推理后训练范式，而非GRPO的低效内存替代方案，具有其独特的优势和适用场景。该研究为理解和应用ES提供了重要的理论基础和实证支持。

Abstract: Evolution Strategies (ES) have recently emerged as a memory-efficient post-training paradigm for LLM reasoning. However, the optimization behavior of ES remains understudied, making it hard to define its advantage scope compared to mainstream post-training paradigms (e.g., Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)). By systematically investigating ES dynamics and mechanisms, this paper first identifies a performance advantage of ES over GRPO, theoretically and empirically showing that ES can lead to broader reasoning coverage, thereby better exploiting the reasoning capabilities of pretrained LLMs. Theoretically, we show that verifier-projected Jensen-Shannon diversity across the ES population is helpful to higher Pass@K performances. Empirically, unlike GRPO, which exhibits entropy collapse, ES improves Pass@1 while attaining higher Pass@K than GRPO. We further develop a sequential GRPO-ES training strategy that combines GRPO's strength in Pass@1 with ES's gains in Pass@K. Second, we find that despite substantial whole-model parameter drift, the task-performance gains of ES are only contributed to a sparse subset of larger-magnitude updates. This functional sparsity suggests that large parameter movement need not imply widespread functional change, and held-out evaluations further show that it does not necessarily lead to catastrophic forgetting. Finally, we study how hyperparameter design affects the effectiveness of ES, demonstrating that ES requires a smaller population size in a larger LLM. These findings position ES as a distinct reasoning post-training paradigm rather than a less effective, memory-efficient alternative to GRPO.

</details>

### [78] [PRO-RAN: Processor-Level Characterization of Open RAN Centralized and Distributed Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26498)
*Moojan Kamalzadeh,Larry Horner,Linqi Xiao,Abhishek Bhattacharyya,Ehsan Bahaloo Horeh,Padmapriya Patil,Venkateswarlu Gudepu,Andrea Fumagalli*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种受控的O-RAN CU/DU功能特征化框架，通过处理器级微架构分析揭示了CU和DU在流量负载下的不同执行特性，为资源调配和优化提供了依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的CPU利用率和吞吐量等性能指标只能量化总体资源使用情况，无法识别函数级执行成本或处理器微架构瓶颈。而处理器级特征化对于O-RAN的资源调配、功能放置、软件优化和硬件加速具有重要意义。

Method: 构建了一个受控特征化框架，集成了Linux Foundation O-CU/DU实现、模拟用户设备、ZeroMQ无线电接口和Open5GS核心网。在匹配的硬件和流量条件下独立部署CU和DU功能。自动化验证确保注册和双向数据包交付后，使用Intel VTune Hotspots和自顶向下微架构分析方法进行进程范围的性能剖析。

Result: 在300秒的剖析间隔内，CU的累积进程CPU时间从17.3秒增加到37.4秒，DU从462.0秒增加到628.4秒。测量结果识别出CU和DU明显的执行特征，证明了函数特定处理器分析和优化的必要性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功建立了O-RAN CU/DU处理器级性能特征化方法，揭示了两者在执行特性和资源需求上的显著差异，为未来的函数级优化、硬件加速和资源管理策略提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Open Radio Access Network (O-RAN) disaggregates RAN protocol functions and enables Centralized Unit (CU) and Distributed Unit (DU) software to execute on general-purpose computing platforms. Different CU and DU protocol responsibilities produce different processor workloads and execution paths. Conventional performance metrics, including CPU utilization and throughput, quantify aggregate resource usage without identifying function-level execution costs or processor microarchitectural bottlenecks. Processor-level characterization, on the other hand, provides insights into resource provisioning, function placement, software optimization, and hardware acceleration. The paper describes a controlled characterization framework that evaluates independently deployed CU and DU functions under matched hardware and traffic conditions. The experimental platform integrates the Linux Foundation OCUDU implementation with an emulated User Equipment, a ZeroMQ-based radio interface, and an Open5GS core. Automated validation confirms registration and bidirectional packet delivery before process-scoped Intel VTune Hotspots and Top-Down Microarchitecture Analysis. Under traffic load, accumulated process CPU time increases from 17.3 s to 37.4 s for the CU and from 462.0 s to 628.4 s for the DU during equal 300-s profiling intervals. The measurements identify distinct CU and DU execution characteristics and motivate function-specific processor analysis and optimization.

</details>


### [79] [SFC-Aware Online Aggregated Data-Link Orchestration for SDN/NFV-Enabled SAGINs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26559)
*Ziyang Guo,Bing Du*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向民航空天地一体化网络的业务功能链感知在线接入侧聚合数据链编排方法(MRSAR)，通过联合编排空间承载资源和时间弹性，在保持高服务成功率的同时实现了良好的质量-复杂度权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 民航空天地一体化网络需要在动态的空对空、空对地和空对卫星数据链路上支持异构的驾驶舱和客舱服务。为了解决这一问题，研究基于SDN/NFV技术的业务功能链感知在线接入侧聚合数据链编排，以实现空间承载资源和时间弹性的联合编排。

Method: 开发了滚动时隙模型，包含四种请求级动作(NOW、TEMP、REJECT、DROP)；采用字典序目标优先级(服务成功→归一化接入编排延迟→TEMP相关风险)；提出模型诱导风险和稀缺性感知精细化(MRSAR)方法，从成功优先目标和数据链/TEMP缓冲区约束中提取延期实现风险和资源稀缺信号，引导基于估值的可行构造和有界邻域精细化。

Result: 仿真结果表明，MRSAR在服务成功率方面接近Gurobi-MILP参考方案，优于到达顺序和延迟近视贪婪基线；能够有效控制REJECT和DROP失败；在滚动在线编排中实现了良好的质量-复杂度权衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的MRSAR方法能够有效解决民航SAGIN中的在线编排问题，避免了全二元动作空间的穷举搜索，在保证服务质量的同时降低了计算复杂度，为实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Civil aviation space-air-ground integrated networks (SAGINs) are expected to support heterogeneous cockpit and cabin services over dynamic air-to-air (A2A), air-to-ground (A2G), and air-to-satellite (A2S) data links. This paper studies service function chain (SFC)-aware online access-side aggregated data-link orchestration for civil aviation SAGINs enabled by software-defined networking and network function virtualization (SDN/NFV). We jointly orchestrate spatial bearer resources and temporal elasticity enabled by temporal elastic mapping and parking (TEMP). A rolling-slot model is developed with four request-level actions: NOW, TEMP, REJECT, and DROP, under a lexicographic objective that prioritizes service success, then normalized access-orchestration delay, and finally residual TEMP-related risk. To avoid exhaustive search over the full binary action space, we propose Model-Induced Risk and Scarcity-Aware Refinement (MRSAR), which derives deferred-realization risk from the success-prioritized objective and resource-scarcity signals from data-link and TEMP buffer constraints. These signals guide valuation-based feasible construction and bounded neighborhood refinement. Simulation results show that MRSAR remains close to the Gurobi-MILP reference in service success, outperforms arrival-order and delay-myopic greedy baselines, controls REJECT and DROP failures, and achieves a favorable quality-complexity tradeoff for rolling online orchestration.

</details>


### [80] [Extending Low Latency Service Across the Internet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26601)
*Harkirat Singh,Fatih Berkay Sarpkaya,Hakan Gulec,Fraida Fund,Shivendra Panwar*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需升级昂贵核心路由器的L4S低延迟服务部署策略，利用BGP communities、SRv6和带速率限制的优先队列等现有网络机制，解决了L4S在非接入链路瓶颈场景下的隔离问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: L4S协议需要L4S流与经典流之间的隔离以保持低延迟优势和安全共存。当前DualPI2 AQM机制仅在家庭接入链路瓶颈处提供隔离，但实际网络中瓶颈也可能出现在对等点、广域网入口或核心网络拥塞链路，而这些位置部署DualPI2不可行。缺乏端到端支持将限制L4S的部署和推广。

Method: 提出一种部署策略，利用现有网络机制（BGP communities、SRv6、带速率限制的优先队列）来提供流隔离，避免升级昂贵的核心路由器。在FABRIC测试平台上进行大规模实验验证方案的可行性。

Result: 实验结果表明该方案可以在整个互联网范围内（包括跨独立管理的网络）实现低延迟服务的实际部署，为L4S的端到端支持提供了可行的解决方案。

Conclusion: 本研究为L4S协议在复杂网络环境中的广泛部署提供了实用的技术路径，即使在不支持DualPI2的核心网络位置也能维持低延迟服务，有助于推动L4S技术在产业界的应用落地。

Abstract: Protocols such as L4S for low latency network services have attracted growing interest from major industry stakeholders such as Comcast, Apple, T-Mobile, and NVIDIA. However, L4S requires isolation between L4S flows and classic flows in order to maintain its low latency benefits and safe coexistence. The L4S architecture uses a DualPI2 AQM mechanism to provide this isolation when the bottleneck occurs at the home access link. In practice, however, bottlenecks may also occur at other locations in the network, such as peering points, ingress to wide area networks, or occasionally congested links in the core, where deploying DualPI2 AQM is not feasible. Without a solution to this problem, L4S may struggle to gain deployment, since its benefits may remain limited without end-to-end support. To address this challenge, we propose a deployment strategy that reduces the need to upgrade expensive core routers by using existing networking mechanisms such as BGP communities, SRv6, and priority queues with rate caps. We demonstrate the feasibility of this approach through large scale experiments on the FABRIC testbed. Our results demonstrate a practical deployment strategy for extending low latency service across the Internet, even across independently administered networks.

</details>


### [81] [Claude Code Complete User Handbook](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26742)
*David Soldani*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Claude Code是一个具有文件系统访问、Shell执行、浏览器控制、外部工具连接和多智能体编排能力的智能体工作环境。该书提出了智能体安全运行的四大核心主张和34章实践指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Claude Code的能力边界已超出单个从业者仅靠注意力就能监管的范围，其失效模式已从局部问题转变为系统性问题（如未审查的钩子、过度范围的连接器、过时的完成条件等），需要为对结果负责的从业者提供安全、高效操作该系统的任务导向型参考。

Method: 提出四个核心命题：(1)没有定义和可观察完成条件的能力不算生产力；(2)指令、权限执行、沙箱和操作系统隔离是控制栈的四个独立层，混淆它们是失控的最常见原因；(3)第三方技能、插件、市场和MCP服务器应作为软件供应链依赖进行治理；(4)智能体工作中正确的信任单元是观察到的证据，而非代理的结案陈述。全书包含从安装到完全验证顶点项目的34章内容，以及治理部分（托管策略、数据驻留和保留、可观察性和可访问性）。

Result: 建立了一个完整的智能体治理框架，包括：每项产品声明均引用原始来源的证据账本；记录循环中声明被证实错误、后续重新验证更改内容及未验证内容的追踪系统；将控制映射到17个外部框架的交叉参考；提出组织采用成熟度模型；对无法从原始来源确认的声明标记为"未验证"而非弱化处理。

Conclusion: 为智能体工作环境的安全高效运行提供了系统性的任务导向参考指南，强调证据驱动的信任机制和分层控制策略，为组织级智能体采用提供了完整的治理框架和成熟度模型。

Abstract: Claude Code is an agentic work environment: a language model operating in a loop with filesystem access, shell execution, browser control, scheduled and cloud execution, external tool connections through the Model Context Protocol, and multi-agent orchestration. Its capability envelope now exceeds what one practitioner can supervise by attention alone, and its failure modes are systemic rather than local: an unreviewed hook, an over-scoped connector, a stale completion condition, an autonomous routine inheriting every credential on an account. This book is a task-oriented reference for operating that system safely and productively, written for practitioners accountable for the result. It advances four propositions. First, capability without a defined and observable completion condition is not productivity. Second, instruction, permission enforcement, sandboxing and operating-system isolation are four distinct layers of a control stack, only two of which are enforced, and conflating them is the most common cause of loss of control. Third, third-party skills, plugins, marketplaces, channels and MCP servers are software supply-chain dependencies and must be governed as such. Fourth, the correct unit of trust in agentic work is observed evidence, not an agent's closing statement. Thirty-four chapters run from installation to a fully verified capstone, with a governance part on managed policy, data residency and retention, observability and accessibility. Every product claim carries a citation to a primary source; an evidence ledger records where a claim in circulation was found wrong, what a later re-verification changed, and what remains unverified. Controls are mapped to seventeen external frameworks in a crosswalk, and an organisational adoption maturity model is proposed. Claims not confirmable from primary sources are labelled UNVERIFIED rather than softened.

</details>


### [82] [FaulT-Bench: Towards Benchmarking Network Troubleshooting LLM Agents under Unreliable User Tickets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27021)
*Kuan-Hao Tseng,Niruth Bogahawatta,Yasod Ginige,Kunjan Patel,Kosta Dakic,Suranga Seneviratne*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出了FaulT-Bench基准测试，包含200个网络故障诊断场景，评估LLM代理在真实场景（包括错误报告、健康网络等）中的表现，发现代理在错误前提的工单下性能急剧下降，工单撰写方式比内容更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM代理网络故障诊断基准仅评估准确工单，且总是假设故障存在，这与实际工作中常见的噪声工单、错误报告等真实条件不符，缺乏对代理在不可靠输入下推理能力的评估。

Method: 构建了包含200个故障诊断场景的FaulT-Bench基准，涵盖8种网络拓扑和4种场景类型（真实故障、虚假故障报告、错误设备归属、错误根因声明）。对72个错误前提工单进行5种报告者角色重写，控制报告者置信度和可验证细节。使用Kathará部署场景，通过NIKA工具接口让代理交互，采用LLM评判器对结果、修复和推理质量评分。

Result: SADE、ReAct和Claude Code三种代理在准确工单上接近饱和，对误导信息具有鲁棒性，但在网络健康且工单错误时性能急剧下降。代理会将良性条件误判为根因而非得出"无故障"结论。工单撰写方式比声称内容更重要：自信的错误报告处理效果接近准确报告，而模糊、未明确的报告会显著降低性能。三种代理失败模式和成本差异显著。

Conclusion: FaulT-Bench为开发能够在现实世界网络故障排查的噪声、不可靠工单上进行可靠推理的代理系统提供了基准，揭示了当前代理在处理错误前提工单时的关键缺陷，为未来改进指明了方向。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly proposed for network fault diagnosis, but existing benchmarks evaluate them only on accurate tickets and always assume a fault is present, conditions rarely met in practice. We present FaulT-Bench, a benchmark of 200 troubleshooting scenarios across eight network topologies, five reimplemented from public practitioner labs, spanning genuine faults, false fault reports, incorrect device attribution, and incorrect root-cause claims. To isolate how ticket wording affects diagnosis, we further rewrite 72 false-premise tickets into five reporter personas that vary reporter confidence and verifiable detail one factor at a time, holding the network state fixed. Our automated harness deploys each scenario in Kathará, lets agents interact through the NIKA tool interface, and scores free-text diagnoses with an LLM judge across outcome, fix, and reasoning quality. Evaluating SADE, ReAct, and Claude Code, we find all three are near-saturated on accurate tickets and robust to misdirection, yet degrade sharply when the network is healthy and the ticket is wrong, probing until a benign condition can be promoted to a root cause rather than concluding nothing is wrong. Persona rewrites show that how a ticket is written matters more than what it claims: a confidently wrong report is handled about as well as an accurate one, while a vague, underspecified report degrades performance sharply. The three agents also fail differently, from constant over-diagnosis to unanswered runs, at very different cost. These results position FaulT-Bench as a benchmark for developing agentic systems that can reason reliably over the noisy, unreliable tickets of real-world network troubleshooting.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

### [83] [Exploring the Role of LLMs in HPC Programming: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26110)
*Strahinja Ljaljevic,Josep Jorba,Sergio Iserte*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本综述系统回顾了大语言模型在高性能计算（HPC）中的应用，分析了其在代码生成、并行化与优化等五大类任务中的表现，指出通用LLM在MPI等分布式范式上存在不足，而领域专用模型虽准确率更高但适用范围狭窄，整体呈现机遇与脆弱性并存的态势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能计算中的编程仍然复杂且需要高度专业化知识，随着大语言模型的快速发展，研究者希望探索LLMs作为HPC编程助手的潜力，系统梳理其应用现状、能力边界及面临的挑战。

Method: 该研究采用系统性文献综述方法，将LLMs在HPC领域的应用划分为五大类别进行分析：代码生成、并行化与优化、框架与架构、评估与基准测试、以及更广泛的挑战，并对比评估通用LLM与领域专用模型（如HPC-Coder、HPC-GPT、chatHPC）的表现。

Result: 研究发现：通用LLM在串行和OpenMP类任务上表现尚可，但在MPI等需要正确性和可扩展性的分布式范式中表现不足；通过微调、精选数据集和检索增强生成（RAG）技术构建的领域专用模型准确性更高，但其适用范围仍较窄，评估主要局限于基准测试或微内核级别。

Conclusion: LLMs短期内不太可能取代HPC专家，但有望成为软件开发流程中的有力协作者；其有效部署需要更丰富的数据集、与性能分析和调度器的集成、严格的评估框架以及确保透明性和信任的治理结构；AI与HPC的融合应被视为一个长期的协同演化过程。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are emerging as promising assistants in High-Performance Computing (HPC), where programming remains complex and expertise-intensive. This survey systematically reviews their application across five categories: code generation, parallelization and optimization, frameworks and architectures, evaluation and benchmarking, and broader challenges. The analysis highlights both opportunities and limitations: while general-purpose LLMs perform reasonably well on serial and OpenMP-like tasks, they fall short in distributed paradigms such as MPI, where correctness and scalability are critical. Domain-specialized models (e.g., HPC-Coder, HPC-GPT, chatHPC) achieve higher accuracy through fine-tuning, curated datasets, and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG), yet their scope remains narrow and their evaluations largely limited to benchmarks or micro-kernels. The broader picture is one of dual potential and fragility: LLMs can lower barriers to entry, accelerate prototyping, and support code modernization, but they remain brittle under production-level requirements where correctness, performance portability, and scaling cannot be compromised.
  We conclude that LLMs are unlikely to replace HPC experts in the near term but are positioned to become powerful collaborators in the software development pipeline. Their effective deployment will require richer datasets, integration with performance analysis and schedulers, rigorous evaluation frameworks, and governance structures that ensure transparency and trust. The convergence of AI and HPC should therefore be understood as a long-term, co-evolutionary process, where each advance uncovers new challenges and opportunities for reshaping scientific software development.

</details>


### [84] [Multi-Dataset Inverse Problem Solving with Distributed Generative AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26283)
*Daniel Lersch,Steven Goldenberg,Johann Rudi,Markus Diefenthaler,Kevin Brager,Xingfu Wu,Yaohang Li,Nobuo Sato*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于生成式AI的广义框架，能够同时分析多个异构数据集，有效提取共享的未知参数，解决了传统方法在数据异质性和计算成本方面的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在科学研究中，经常需要从多个异构数据集（例如不同设置下测量的数据）中提取共享的未知量。独立分析或简单合并数据会导致估计有偏或不准确，而联合分析需要谨慎处理数据异质性且计算成本高，因此需要一种能够同时处理多个异构数据集的有效框架。

Method: 基于SAGIPS（可扩展异步生成逆问题求解器）框架，将分布式数据并行训练范式扩展到非独立同分布数据集。每个数据集拥有独立的前向算子和判别器，通过共同的生成器学习共享的未知推理参数，各数据集提供互补约束，共同引导生成器实现全局参数一致性。

Result: 在受控的多探测器散射实验设置中验证了该方法。数值结果表明，该框架对由未知探测器系统误差引起的不同数据保真度具有鲁棒性，并展示了在多GPU高性能计算系统上的良好扩展性能。

Conclusion: 该方法非常适合实验条件在不同测量间变化的现实世界多数据集分析，为异构数据集的联合分析提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Extracting a shared set of unknown, not directly measurable quantities from multiple, heterogeneous datasets is a common challenge across scientific domains. A prominent example is the combination of datasets obtained from different measurements with different settings (e.g. varying detector resolutions). Analyzing such datasets jointly, rather than independently or after naive merging, is essential for obtaining precise and unbiased estimates of the unknowns, but requires careful treatment of dataset heterogeneity and is computationally demanding. We present a generalized framework for simultaneously analyzing multiple heterogeneous datasets in the context of generative AI-based inverse problem solvers. Building on our recent Scalable Asynchronous Generative Inverse Problem Solver (SAGIPS) framework, we extend the well-established distributed data-parallel training paradigm to non-identically distributed datasets, where each dataset is controlled by the same set of unknown inference parameters but covers a different region of the available feature space. Each dataset is processed through its own forward operator and discriminator, providing complementary constraints that collectively guide a shared generator toward global parameter consistency. We validate the approach using a controlled setup inspired by a multi-detector scattering experiment. We provide numerical evidence that our framework is robust to different data fidelities, which arise from unknown detector systematics in the Rutherford experiment, and we show the scaling behavior on multi-GPU leadership computing systems. The results show that our approach is well suited for real-world multi-dataset analyses in which experimental conditions vary across measurements.

</details>


### [85] [VPP: Virtual Pipeline Parallelism for Efficient Chunked Prefill in Long-Context LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26523)
*Yan Shi,Xiaochao Wang,Jingchun Gao,Jintao Luo,Xinyi Zhou,Feng Liu,Kui Luo,Xushi Li,Xinjie Guo,Liangjun Feng*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文针对大模型推理中分块预填充流水线并行（CPP）存在的负载不均衡问题，提出了虚拟流水线并行（VPP）方法。通过V形虚拟阶段遍历和异步通信优化，VPP在长序列上实现了高达13.1%的吞吐量提升，并将流水线气泡率降低了98.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CPP方法中，固定大小的分块会导致流水线负载不均衡，因为后续分块需要处理更长的前缀KV缓存，导致注意力计算成本增加，产生流水线气泡。虽然动态调整分块大小的DCPP方法可以缓解这一问题，但实验测量表明，这种方法在长序列上会引入额外的调度开销，导致整体性能下降。

Method: 本文提出虚拟流水线并行（VPP）技术，保持分块大小固定，通过虚拟阶段优化流水线布局。核心设计包括：（1）V形虚拟阶段遍历，将每个分块计算密集的中间阶段与相邻分块的轻量级头尾阶段重叠执行；（2）异步通信机制减少通信停顿；（3）流水线打包技术降低跨请求的排空气泡。该方法在vLLM-Ascend框架中实现。

Result: 在16个Ascend 910C NPU上，使用三个基于MoE的大语言模型进行评估，序列长度最高达1M tokens。VPP在长序列上相比DCPP提升吞吐量最高达13.1%，在混合工作负载上提升6.7%，同时在短序列上保持性能不下降。在512K tokens的DeepSeek-V3.1预填充任务中，VPP将流水线气泡率从6.4%降至0.1%，相比DCPP降低了98.0%。

Conclusion: VPP通过创新的虚拟阶段设计和流水线优化策略，有效解决了传统CPP方法中的负载不均衡问题，避免了动态调整带来的调度开销，在长序列推理场景中显著提升了性能。该方法为大模型长序列推理提供了一种高效、可扩展的流水线并行解决方案。

Abstract: Chunked prefill pipeline parallelism (CPP) is a key technique for LLM inference. However, equal-size chunks exhibit imbalanced latency, as later chunks attend longer prefix KV caches and incur higher attention costs, leading to pipeline bubbles. Existing approaches mitigate this imbalance through dynamic chunk resizing (Dynamic CPP, DCPP), but our measurements show that this trades scheduling overhead for load balancing, which becomes unfavorable on long sequences. In this study, we propose Virtual Pipeline Parallelism (VPP), which keeps chunk sizes fixed and optimizes the pipeline layout through virtual stages. A V-shaped virtual-stage traversal overlaps each chunk's expensive middle stages with the lighter head and tail stages of its neighbors, while asynchronous communication and pipelined packing further reduce communication stalls and cross-request drain bubbles. We implement VPP in vLLM-Ascend and evaluate it on three MoE-based LLMs with sequences up to 1M tokens on 16 Ascend 910C NPUs. VPP improves throughput by up to 13.1% over DCPP on long sequences and 6.7% on mixed workloads, while preserving performance on short sequences. On a 512K-token DeepSeek-V3.1 prefill workload, VPP reduces the pipeline bubble ratio from 6.4% to 0.1%, achieving a 98.0% reduction compared with DCPP.

</details>


### [86] [Optimizing API Gateway Placement in Multi-Cloud Kubernetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26573)
*Vinoth Punniyamoorthy,Murali Shankar Dulam,Aswathnarayan Muthukrishnan Kirubakaran,Akshay Deshpande,Nachiappan Chockalingam,Bikesh Kumar,Naga Surya Pasupuleti,Narender Reddy Bitla*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究地理分布式多云Kubernetes集群中的API网关放置优化问题，提出MILP精确模型和贪心启发式算法，在保证延迟约束下降低基础设施成本。贪心算法相对最优解误差3.2%-4.7%，计算速度提升660-3490倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在地理分布式多云Kubernetes集群环境中部署API网关存在基础设施成本、计算资源和网络延迟之间的权衡问题。现有方法要么全量复制导致成本过高，要么单一部署无法满足延迟要求，需要一种优化方法来平衡成本与性能。

Method: 将API网关放置问题建模为有容量设施选址问题，同时确定激活哪些候选集群、部署多少网关副本以及如何分配流量。提出两种求解方法：(1)混合整数线性规划(MILP)精确模型；(2)基于增量成本排序的构造性贪心启发式算法。模型包含客户端到集群往返延迟上限约束和网关副本容量预留因子。

Result: 在3-12个候选集群的合成测试实例上，贪心算法相对MILP最优解的误差为3.2%-4.7%，最大误差25.0%，计算速度提升660-3490倍。在10候选集群、10需求区域的基准场景中，MILP最优部署比全量复制基线节省24.2%月度成本。选择单一最便宜集群虽可额外节省24.8%但无法满足3个需求区域的延迟要求。

Conclusion: 所提优化方法能有效平衡成本与延迟约束，MILP提供最优解，贪心算法在可接受误差范围内大幅提升计算效率。研究结果为多云环境下的API网关部署提供了理论指导和实用工具。

Abstract: The use of API gateways within geographically distributed multi-cloud Kubernetes clusters poses a tradeoff between infrastructure cost, computational resources, and network latencies. We present an optimization formulation that addresses API gateway placement as a capacitated facility location problem that jointly determines which candidate clusters to activate, how many gateway replicas to deploy, and how regional traffic should be distributed across the selected clusters. The formulation imposes an upper bound on estimated client-to-cluster network round-trip latency, excluding gateway processing, queuing, and backendservice latency, and incorporates a utilization headroom factor for gateway replica capacity. We present both a mixed-integer linear programming (MILP) formulation and a constructive greedy heuristic that ranks candidates according to incremental cost, comprising cluster-activation and marginal replica costs, per unit of assignable capacity while explicitly accounting for already committed load. Both formulations are applied to deterministic, seed-controlled, geography-based synthetic instances. For each problem size, 30 instances are generated with random seeds to analyze their performance. The greedy algorithm achieves an optimality gap of 3.2% to 4.7% to the MILP optimal solution, with a maximum observed gap of 25.0% for one particular instance, and a speedup of approximately 660x to 3,490x for 3 to 12 candidate clusters. In a canonical 10-candidate, 10-demand region instance, MILP-optimal deployment saves 24.2% in terms of monthly cost compared to the full-replication baseline. On the other hand, selecting the single cheapest candidate yields savings of 24.8% compared to the MILP optimum but does not satisfy the latency requirement for 3 out of 10 demand regions.

</details>


### [87] [Benchmarking Confidential Computing Performance on NVIDIA Blackwell GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26575)
*Daniyal Khan,Amean Asad,Ansgar Grunseid*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文评估了在NVIDIA B200 GPU上使用Intel TDX和NVIDIA机密计算(CC)运行大语言模型推理和训练的性能开销。研究发现正确配置的机密推理仅产生1-3%的吞吐量开销，而默认配置会产生30-40%的可避免性能损失。开销由主机操作固定成本和NVLink加密通信成本两个独立因素决定，GPU计算、能耗和可用内存容量不受CC影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在敏感场景中的广泛应用，保护模型和数据隐私成为关键需求。可信执行环境(TEE)提供硬件级安全保障，但其实际部署中的性能开销缺乏系统性研究。本研究旨在量化NVIDIA Blackwell架构下机密计算的真实性能影响，为实际部署提供参考依据。

Method: 在单个物理主机上进行配对实验，对比机密与非机密运行场景，唯一变量为GPU CC标志位和TDX访客对象。系统性地分析性能开销来源，将其分解为两个独立维度：随批量增长而摊销的固定主机操作成本，以及与NVLink加密集合通信时间成比例的通信成本。通过微基准测试精确定位各加密边界处的开销。

Result: 正确配置的机密推理吞吐量开销仅为1-3%（低个位数），而默认推理堆栈因可避免的配置问题产生30-40%的性能损失。性能开销由两个独立因素决定，且可通过工作负载和软件配置进行优化。GPU计算性能、能耗和可用内存容量不受机密计算影响。所提出的微基准测试能够精确预测推理性能损失。

Conclusion: NVIDIA Blackwell GPU上的机密计算能够以极低的性能开销(1-3%)实现大语言模型的安全推理，前提是正确配置推理堆栈。论文提供了具体的部署指导建议，为在实际生产环境中采用机密计算保护大语言模型提供了可行的技术路径和性能预期。

Abstract: This paper measures the performance impact of running large language model inference and training inside a Trusted Execution Environment (TEE) on NVIDIA B200 GPUs, using Intel Trust Domain Extensions (TDX) confidential VMs together with NVIDIA Confidential Computing (CC) on Blackwell GPUs. The performance impact is derived from paired confidential versus non-confidential runs on a single physical host where the only variable is the GPU CC bit and the TDX guest object in the VM launch. The main result is that confidential inference on Blackwell achieves low single-digit throughput overhead when the stack is configured correctly, at about 1-3%. Stock inference stacks incur 30 to 40% penalties due to avoidable configurations rather than the achievable operating point. The cost is not fully represented by a single number because it is governed by two independent axes, a fixed per-host-operation cost that amortizes as batch size grows and a per-NVLink-traffic cost that tracks the share of the step spent in encrypted collectives, and which of the two dominates is set by the workload and the software. We localize each cost to a specific encrypted boundary, give a microbenchmark that predicts the serving penalty to within a submission count, and end with concrete deployment guidance. GPU compute, energy draw, and usable memory capacity are unaffected by CC.

</details>


### [88] [Sintr: Safe Interactive Transactions in the Presence of Byzantine Clients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27091)
*Austin T. Li,Daniel H. Lee,Lorenzo Alvisi,Natacha Crooks,Florian Suri-Payer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: Sintr框架通过冗余执行和策略框架解决了BFT数据库中恶意客户端威胁数据库完整性的问题，且仅带来3%-21%的性能开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BFT数据库系统采用客户端中心架构提升性能和开发便利性，但引入了关键漏洞：恶意客户端可能发起错误交易威胁数据库完整性。

Method: 提出Sintr框架，结合交易结果冗余执行验证机制与灵活的异构策略框架，用于表达和强制执行应用的数据完整性要求。

Result: 将Sintr应用于多个现有BFT数据库系统，结果显示性能开销较低：吞吐量下降3%-16%，延迟增加3%-21%。

Conclusion: Sintr有效防御了恶意客户端对BFT数据库完整性的威胁，同时保持了可接受的性能开销，成功平衡了安全性与性能之间的权衡。

Abstract: Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) systems are, in principle, an appealing foundation for transactional applications involving mutually distrustful participants. Yet their adoption has been hampered by two persistent stumbling blocks-performance and developer convenience-which are often in tension with one another. Recent systems show promising progress on both fronts by shifting to a client-centric architecture; clients execute transactions locally and concurrently, while the system resolves any data conflicts to maintain database serializability. We argue that, in its current form, this approach introduces a critical vulnerability: it leaves the integrity of the database exposed to Byzantine clients, which may issue malicious or incorrect transactions. We address this threat with Sintr, a framework that prevents Byzantine clients from compromising database integrity by executing rogue transactions. Sintr combines redundant execution to validate transaction outcomes with a flexible, heterogeneous policy framework for expressing an application's data-integrity requirements. We apply Sintr to harden several existing BFT database systems and find that it imposes only modest overheads-3%-16% in throughput and 3%-21% in latency.

</details>


### [89] [Decoupled I/O-Dominant Pipelines for Large-Scale Whole-Slide Image Embedding Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27278)
*Mayanka Chandrashekar,Xi Zhang,Ethan Seefried,Tirthankar Ghosal,John Gounley,Heidi Hanson*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个解耦的I/O感知流程，用于大规模全切片图像（WSI）嵌入提取，通过将I/O、计算和数据摄入分离，解决了大规模病理图像处理中的性能瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片图像（WSIs）在计算病理学中至关重要，但其巨大尺寸使得基于补丁的处理成为基础模型推理的实际单元。然而，大规模处理会产生海量补丁，带来显著的I/O和协调开销，往往主导端到端性能，成为系统瓶颈。

Method: 提出了一个解耦的、I/O感知的WSI嵌入提取流程，将工作流分解为三个阶段：（1）补丁生成与暂存；（2）高度并行的嵌入推理；（3）分片向量数据库摄入。该设计将数据移动与计算隔离，实现高效补丁交付、可扩展的多节点推理和最小化通信。最终系统生成分布式向量数据库，嵌入与丰富元数据（如患者、切片和补丁属性）持久耦合。

Result: 证明了I/O、计算和摄入的解耦能够实现高通量的大规模WSI嵌入提取。通过刻画扩展边界，发现在中等并发度以上存储成为主导因素，将WSI嵌入提取重新定义为以数据为中心的系统问题，而非纯计算受限的工作负载。生成的紧凑可重用表示数据库可支持检索、分类和少样本学习等任务，特别有利于低资源环境。

Conclusion: 将WSI嵌入提取从计算密集型问题重新定位为以数据为中心的系统问题，通过流程解耦和I/O优化实现了大规模高效处理，为计算病理学中的基础模型应用提供了可扩展的系统解决方案。

Abstract: Whole-slide images (WSIs) are central to computational pathology but are prohibitively large, making patch-based processing the practical unit for foundation model inference. At scale, however, generating and handling massive numbers of patches on quickly introduces significant I/O and orchestration overhead, often dominating end-to-end performance. We present a decoupled, I/O-aware pipeline for large-scale WSI embedding extraction that decomposes the workflow into three stages: (1) patch generation and staging, (2) embarrassingly parallel embedding inference, and (3) sharded vector database ingestion. This design isolates data movement from compute, enabling efficient patch delivery, scalable multi-node inference with minimal communication. The resulting system produces a distributed vector database where embeddings are persistently coupled with rich metadata (e.g., patient, slide, and patch attributes), enabling efficient filtering, retrieval, and downstream reuse. This representation database is compact and reusable for tasks such as retrieval, classification, and few-shot learning, particularly benefiting low-resource environments. We show that decoupling I/O, computation, and ingestion enables high-throughput WSI embedding extraction at scale. By characterizing the scaling envelope, we demonstrate that storage dominates beyond moderate concurrency, reframing WSI embedding extraction as a data-centric systems problem rather than a purely compute-bound workload.

</details>


### [90] [Consensus with Stochastic Broadcast](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.27336)
*Pierre Fraigniaud,Boaz Patt-Shamir,Sergio Rajsbaum*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究随机广播模型下的二进制共识问题，将以往针对2个进程的研究扩展到n>2个进程的通用情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在随机广播模型中，每个进程每轮广播消息成功的概率为p，发送者无法获知广播是否成功，导致共识问题不可解。研究目标是设计在固定轮数r内终止的共识算法，最小化错误分歧的概率。此前研究仅针对2个进程，本文旨在扩展到更通用的n>2进程场景。

Method: 采用随机广播模型，假设n≥2个进程通过消息广播进行同步通信。每个进程在每轮广播一个消息，广播以独立概率p成功（所有进程接收）或失败（无进程接收）。针对给定轮数r，设计在r轮内终止的共识算法。

Result: 成功将DISC 2025中针对2个进程的二进制共识研究扩展到n>2个进程的情况，实现了模型的泛化研究。

Conclusion: 本研究推动了随机广播模型下共识问题理论边界的拓展，为多进程分布式系统中的容错共识算法设计提供了新的理论基础。

Abstract: We study binary consensus in the \emph{stochastic broadcast model}, which assumes $n\geq 2$ processes communicating synchronously by message broadcasts. At each round, every process broadcasts a message to all the other processes. Each broadcast succeeds independently with some probability $p\in[0,1]$. If a broadcast succeeds, all processes receive the message, and if it fails, no process receives the message. The sender does not know whether its broadcast was successful or not. In this model, consensus is not solvable; the objective is to design, for a given number of rounds $r$, consensus algorithms that terminate in $r$ rounds, minimizing the probability of error disagreement. This problem has been studied in depth for 2 processes [DISC 2025]. We extend the study to $n> 2$.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [91] [Zero-Shot Self-Orchestration with Ledger-Based Control for Improved LLM Coding Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26480)
*Victor Gao,Vida Khosrowshahi,Ali Khosrowshahi,Xihao Sun,Juhyun Lee,Simon,Lee*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 在无训练、无针对性调优的条件下，引入管理器-工作者架构对多智能体LLM系统的提升是真实但有条件的：部分模型（如Qwen3.8-27B、GPT-5.6-Luna/Terra、Kimi-K3、Minimax-M3）增益显著（+8至+42），而其他模型（如Qwen3.6-35B）无增益甚至负增益。运行管理器约使Token成本增加三倍，但比升级到更大模型更具成本效益（GPT-5.6-Terra+管理器达到Fable 5单次调用约97%的准确率，成本仅为其1/5）。增益机制主要为上下文管理和问题分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究声称多智能体LLM系统优于单模型基线，但证据混杂，且对比通常存在混淆因素：流水线同时改变Token预算、工具调用和提示词，导致整体增益难以揭示实际起作用的因素。因此，本文旨在隔离性地研究引入管理器-工作者架构对共享文件系统工作空间的影响，以明确架构本身的真实效果。

Method: 引入管理器-工作者架构到共享文件系统工作空间，无需训练、无需针对基准测试调优，与相同模型的单次直接回答进行对比。在9个模型（5个开源模型，参数量从9B到约2.8T；4个前沿闭源模型）上测试100道LiveCodeBench最新难题。采用配对测试，部分在推理模式开启/关闭下分别测试。同时进行Transcript分析以识别增益机制。

Result: 架构增益具有条件性：(1)显著正向：Qwen3.8-27B (+23.4)、GPT-5.6-Luna (+10.6)、GPT-5.6-Terra (+8.0)；Kimi-K3 (+30.4)和Minimax-M3 (+11.0)在推理关闭时（p<10⁻⁴），单次测试中Kimi-K3达+42；(2)无增益或负增益：Qwen3.6-35B (-1到-9)。(3)成本效益：GPT-5.6-Terra+管理器（85.0%准确率，$11.71/100题）接近Fable 5单次调用（87.4%，$61.11/100题），成本仅1/5。(4)Opus-5配合管理器在单次测试中达到91%最高分。增益机制主要为上下文管理和问题分解。

Conclusion: 管理器-工作者架构对多智能体LLM系统的提升真实存在但非普适，效果依赖于模型特性（如推理模式开启/关闭、模型规模）。尽管运行管理器约使Token成本增加三倍，但相比升级到更大模型，它能以更低成本获得更高准确率。研究表明，架构设计需针对特定模型和应用场景进行评估和优化。

Abstract: Multi-agent large language model systems are widely reported to beat single-model baselines, but the evidence is mixed, and comparisons are usually confounded: pipelines change token budgets, tool calls, and prompts simultaneously, so an aggregate gain rarely reveals what actually helped.
